proved Read and re-derived the entire bordism-over-G proof. The actual pair of weighted Hopf maps yields the smooth torus orbibundle N, and its hyperbolic pullback has universal cover H2 times R2. The literal quotient maps send all six generators identically, giving the required classifying map. The primitive character, Euler number and duality give integral homology(S1 times S3). Davis–Kirk Definition8.16/Theorem8.17 pp209–210 and IWX Corollary1.3/Table1 pp112–113 were opened. The bounding sphere framings give components(0,eta^3); the product vanishes because eta^4=0. Relative transversality of a framed13-bordism against N gives the actual framed11-filling over BG, and the normal framing pulled from the first S3 matches the prescribed one. The group and Euler certificate was replayed; no smooth classification is inferred. Source proofs and historical checks were available; this is a re-derivation, not a blind check.

proved Complete unbounded-family, all-lattice obstruction and rational (5,4) normal form Read all six full proofs. Re-derived the individual finite-action complex structures from even real eigenspaces; XY nilpotence and Y(A+I)^(-1)X=I; symbolic integral overlattice and primitive cusp image; the nonselfdual pencil determinant obstruction; the q=v^2-v+1 torsion in the specified lattice; and the residual subobject/quotient dichotomy for every invariant lattice at primes outside 2,5. The four-dimensional residual pair is absolutely irreducible: one-dimensional factors force simultaneous orders 5 and 4 to be 1, and a 2+2 split forces a -I block incompatible with its unipotent quotient. For v=1 the explicit order-four monomial conjugacy and theta=a+4k marking give torsion d2 residue 1 in the retained degrees. For the rational normal form, the exact centralizer action gives the projective beta parameter, the ten linear constraints have the displayed cubic determinant, positive Q1 and the two adjugate rows isolate both exceptional projective points; one is inconsistent and the other is the complete affine solution line. The three current unchanged programs supplement, rather than replace, these geometric and universal arguments.
